+"""Focused safety tests; all host commands are mocked."""
+import contextlib
+import importlib
+import io
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import stat
+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+from unittest.mock import patch
+
+try:
+    helper = importlib.import_module('crowdsec_existing_deployment')
+except ModuleNotFoundError:
+    helper = None
+
+
+class ExistingDeploymentTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.assertIsNotNone(helper, 'existing deployment helper is not implemented')
+        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
+        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
+        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)
+        self.backup = self.root / 'attempt'
+        self.backup.mkdir(mode=0o700)
+        self.config = self.root / 'bouncer.yaml'
+        self.config.write_text('mode: iptables\napi_key: secret-canary\n')
+        self.enter(patch.object(helper.os, 'geteuid', return_value=0))
+        # Only fixture ownership is relaxed; production still requires root.
+        self.enter(patch.object(helper, '_root_owned', side_effect=lambda info: info.st_uid in (0, self.root.stat().st_uid)))
+        self.command = self.enter(patch.object(helper.subprocess, 'run'))
+        self.current = b'MAILTO=private@example.test\n# keep\n@reboot /sbin/iptables-restore < /etc/iptables/rules.v4\n0 * * * * echo private\n@reboot ip6tables-restore < /etc/iptables/rules.v6\n'
+        self.writes = []
+        self.command.side_effect = self.crontab
+
+    def enter(self, manager):
+        value = manager.__enter__()
+        self.addCleanup(manager.__exit__, None, None, None)
+        return value
+
+    def crontab(self, argv, **kwargs):
+        self.assertEqual(argv[:3], ['crontab', '-u', 'root'])
+        self.assertNotIn('shell', kwargs)
+        self.assertEqual(kwargs['timeout'], 30)
+        if argv[3:] == ['-l']:
+            return subprocess.CompletedProcess(argv, 0, self.current, b'')
+        self.assertEqual(argv[3:], ['-'])
+        self.current = kwargs['input']
+        self.writes.append(self.current)
+        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(argv, 0, b'', b'')
+
+    def test_exact_recognition_and_sanitized_line_indexes(self):
+        self.current += b'@reboot /usr/sbin/ip6tables-restore </etc/iptables/rules.v6\n'
+        rows = helper.cron_list()
+        self.assertEqual([r['index'] for r in rows], [3, 5, 6])
+        self.assertEqual([r['family'] for r in rows], ['IPv4', 'IPv6', 'IPv6'])
+        self.assertNotIn('private', json.dumps(rows))
+        self.assertEqual(self.writes, [])
+
+    def test_unknown_commands_are_never_recognized(self):
+        commands = ['iptables-restore --noflush < /etc/iptables/rules.v4',
+                    'iptables-restore < /etc/iptables/rules.v6',
+                    'iptables-restore < /etc/iptables/rules.v4; echo secret',
+                    'iptables-restore < /etc/iptables/rules.v4 # note',
+                    'env iptables-restore < /etc/iptables/rules.v4',
+                    './iptables-restore < /etc/iptables/rules.v4',
+                    '/tmp/../sbin/iptables-restore < /etc/iptables/rules.v4',
+                    'iptables-restore < /etc/iptables/rules.v4 > /tmp/out']
+        self.current = ''.join('@reboot '+c+'\n' for c in commands).encode()
+        self.assertEqual(helper.cron_list(), [])
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(list(self.backup.iterdir()), [])
+        self.assertEqual(self.writes, [])
+
+    def test_disable_and_restore_preserve_all_other_bytes_and_private_backups(self):
+        original = self.current
+        helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 3)
+        after = original.replace(b'@reboot /sbin/', b'# CrowdBastion disabled: @reboot /sbin/', 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.current, after)
+        self.assertEqual((self.backup/'root.crontab.before').read_bytes(), original)
+        self.assertEqual((self.backup/'root.crontab.after').read_bytes(), after)
+        for path in self.backup.iterdir():
+            self.assertEqual(stat.S_IMODE(path.stat().st_mode), 0o600)
+        self.assertEqual(json.loads((self.backup/'cron.json').read_text())['index'], 3)
+        helper.cron_restore(self.backup)
+        self.assertEqual(self.current, original)
+        self.assertEqual(self.writes, [after, original])
+
+    def test_restore_refuses_external_changes(self):
+        helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 3)
+        self.current += b'# external edit\n'
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            helper.cron_restore(self.backup)
+        self.assertEqual(len(self.writes), 1)
+
+    def test_disable_rereads_and_refuses_concurrent_changes(self):
+        reads = 0
+        def changed(argv, **kwargs):
+            nonlocal reads
+            if argv[-1] == '-l':
+                reads += 1
+                if reads == 2:
+                    self.current += b'# concurrent\n'
+            return self.crontab(argv, **kwargs)
+        self.command.side_effect = changed
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 3)
+        self.assertEqual(reads, 2)
+        self.assertEqual(self.writes, [])
+
+    def test_unreadable_crontab_is_an_error(self):
+        for code, stderr in [(1, b'permission denied secret'), (2, b'error')]:
+            with self.subTest(code=code, stderr=stderr):
+                self.command.return_value = subprocess.CompletedProcess([], code, b'', stderr)
+                self.command.side_effect = None
+                with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as caught:
+                    helper.cron_list()
+                self.assertNotIn('secret', str(caught.exception))
+                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+                    helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(list(self.backup.iterdir()), [])
+
+    def test_confirmed_absent_root_crontab_has_no_candidates(self):
+        self.command.side_effect = None
+        self.command.return_value = subprocess.CompletedProcess([], 1, b'', b'no crontab for root\n')
+        self.assertEqual(helper.cron_list(), [])
+
+    def test_invalid_indexes_never_write(self):
+        for index in (0, -1, 1, 2, 4, 6, True):
+            with self.subTest(index=index), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+                helper.cron_disable(self.backup, index)
+        self.assertEqual(self.writes, [])
+        self.assertEqual(list(self.backup.iterdir()), [])
+
+    def test_backup_reuse_is_refused_without_overwriting(self):
+        helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 3)
+        saved = {p.name: p.read_bytes() for p in self.backup.iterdir()}
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 5)
+        self.assertEqual(saved, {p.name: p.read_bytes() for p in self.backup.iterdir()})
+        self.assertEqual(len(self.writes), 1)
+
+    def test_unsafe_backup_paths_refused(self):
+        link = self.root/'link'
+        link.symlink_to(self.backup, target_is_directory=True)
+        for path in (Path('relative'), link, link/'child', self.root/'missing'):
+            with self.subTest(path=path), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+                helper.cron_disable(path, 3)
+        self.backup.chmod(0o755)
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 3)
+        self.assertEqual(self.writes, [])
+
+    def test_untrusted_ancestor_and_nonroot_refused(self):
+        with patch.object(helper, '_root_owned', return_value=False):
+            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+                helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 3)
+        with patch.object(helper.os, 'geteuid', return_value=1000):
+            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+                helper.cron_list()
+        self.assertEqual(self.writes, [])
+
+    def test_restore_rejects_symlink_or_public_backup(self):
+        helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 3)
+        before = self.backup/'root.crontab.before'
+        before.chmod(0o644)
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            helper.cron_restore(self.backup)
+        before.unlink()
+        before.symlink_to(self.config)
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            helper.cron_restore(self.backup)
+        self.assertEqual(len(self.writes), 1)
+
+    def test_install_failure_keeps_recovery_records(self):
+        original = self.crontab
+        def fail(argv, **kwargs):
+            if argv[-1] == '-':
+                return subprocess.CompletedProcess(argv, 1, b'', b'secret')
+            return original(argv, **kwargs)
+        self.command.side_effect = fail
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as caught:
+            helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 3)
+        self.assertNotIn('secret', str(caught.exception))
+        self.assertTrue((self.backup/'root.crontab.before').is_file())
+
+    def test_timeout_after_install_can_restore_without_overwriting_others(self):
+        original = self.current
+        original_command = self.crontab
+        def interrupted(argv, **kwargs):
+            result = original_command(argv, **kwargs)
+            if argv[-1] == '-':
+                raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired(argv, 30)
+            return result
+        self.command.side_effect = interrupted
+        with self.assertRaises(subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
+            helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 3)
+        self.command.side_effect = original_command
+        helper.cron_restore(self.backup)
+        self.assertEqual(self.current, original)
+
+
+    def test_absolute_binary_at_root_and_unterminated_line(self):
+        self.current = b'@reboot /iptables-restore < /etc/iptables/rules.v4'
+        self.assertEqual(helper.cron_list(), [{'index': 1, 'family': 'IPv4'}])
+        before = self.current
+        helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 1)
+        helper.cron_restore(self.backup)
+        self.assertEqual(self.current, before)
+
+    def test_carriage_return_is_not_a_crontab_line_boundary(self):
+        self.current = b'@reboot iptables-restore < /etc/iptables/rules.v4\rmalicious\n'
+        self.assertEqual(helper.cron_list(), [])
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.writes, [])
+
+    def test_unrelated_snapshots_are_untouched(self):
+        snapshot = self.backup/'rules.v4'
+        snapshot.write_bytes(b'snapshot-canary')
+        snapshot.chmod(0o640)
+        helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 3)
+        helper.cron_restore(self.backup)
+        self.assertEqual(snapshot.read_bytes(), b'snapshot-canary')
+        self.assertEqual(stat.S_IMODE(snapshot.stat().st_mode), 0o640)
+
+    def test_writable_nonsticky_ancestor_is_refused(self):
+        self.root.chmod(0o777)
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 3)
+        self.assertEqual(self.writes, [])
+
+    def test_restore_rejects_tampered_transition(self):
+        helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 3)
+        (self.backup/'root.crontab.before').write_bytes(b'@reboot echo attacker\n')
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            helper.cron_restore(self.backup)
+        self.assertEqual(len(self.writes), 1)
+
+    def test_empty_read_success_is_empty_but_read_failure_never_is(self):
+        self.current = b''
+        self.assertEqual(helper.cron_list(), [])
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            helper.cron_disable(self.backup, 1)
+        self.assertEqual(self.writes, [])
+
+    def probes(self, coverage='fault'):
+        self.enter(patch.object(helper.runtime, 'cron_risks', return_value=['/etc/rc.local:2']))
+        self.enter(patch.object(helper.runtime, 'restore_services', return_value=['iptables.service']))
+        docker = self.enter(patch.object(helper.runtime, 'docker_present', return_value=True))
+        chains = self.enter(patch.object(helper.runtime, 'chain_coverage', return_value=coverage))
+        return docker, chains
+
+    def test_plan_uses_merged_config_and_runtime_with_actionable_scope(self):
+        self.config.write_text('mode: nftables\napi_key: secret-canary\n')
+        Path(str(self.config)+'.local').write_text('mode: iptables\n')
+        docker, chains = self.probes()
+        report = helper.plan(self.config)
+        self.assertEqual(report['chain_coverage'], 'fault')
+        self.assertTrue(report['docker_present'])
+        self.assertEqual(report['cron_risks'], ['/etc/rc.local:2'])
+        self.assertEqual(report['restore_services'], ['iptables.service'])
+        self.assertIn('DOCKER-USER', json.dumps(report))
+        self.assertIn('FORWARD', json.dumps(report))
+        self.assertNotIn('secret-canary', json.dumps(report))
+        docker.assert_called_once_with()
+        chains.assert_called_once_with(self.config, True)
+        self.assertEqual(self.writes, [])
+
+    def test_plan_refuses_unsupported_unreadable_or_unknown(self):
+        self.probes('unknown')
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            helper.plan(self.config)
+        self.config.write_text('mode: nftables\n')
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            helper.plan(self.config)
+        self.config.unlink()
+        with self.assertRaises((ValueError, OSError)):
+            helper.plan(self.config)
+
+    def test_plan_probe_errors_fail_closed(self):
+        self.probes()
+        with patch.object(helper.runtime, 'cron_risks', side_effect=PermissionError('secret')):
+            with self.assertRaises((ValueError, OSError)):
+                helper.plan(self.config)
+
+    def test_cli_dispatch_and_sanitized_errors(self):
+        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()) as output:
+            self.assertEqual(helper.main(['cron-list']), 0)
+        self.assertEqual(json.loads(output.getvalue())[0]['index'], 3)
+        self.assertNotIn('private', output.getvalue())
+        self.assertEqual(helper.main(['cron-disable', str(self.backup), '3']), 0)
+        self.assertEqual(helper.main(['cron-restore', str(self.backup)]), 0)
+        self.probes()
+        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()) as output:
+            self.assertEqual(helper.main(['plan', str(self.config)]), 0)
+        self.assertIn('scope', json.loads(output.getvalue()))
+        self.command.side_effect = PermissionError('private-canary')
+        with contextlib.redirect_stderr(io.StringIO()) as errors:
+            self.assertEqual(helper.main(['cron-list']), 1)
+        self.assertNotIn('private-canary', errors.getvalue())
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    unittest.main()
+

+"""Existing-deployment navigation uses isolated system-command stubs."""
+import unittest
+import test_workflows
+
+
+class ExistingWorkflow(unittest.TestCase):
+    setUp = test_workflows.Workflows.setUp
+    run_shell = test_workflows.Workflows.run_shell
+    commands = test_workflows.Workflows.commands
+
+    def stubs(self):
+        return '''
+source "$SCRIPT_DIR/lib/firewall.sh"
+existing_deployment_tool() {
+ record existing "$@"
+ case "$1" in
+ plan) printf '{"backend":"iptables","cron_risks":[],"restore_services":[],"scope":[]}' ;;
+ cron-list) printf '[]' ;;
+ esac
+}
+repair_firewall_startup() { record repair-startup; }
+check_firewall_runtime() { record check-runtime; return 0; }
+'''
+
+    def test_existing_repair_cancel_and_eof_do_not_modify(self):
+        for inputs in ('n\n', ''):
+            self.trace.write_text('')
+            p = self.run_shell('repair_existing_deployment', inputs, self.stubs())
+            self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout+p.stderr)
+            self.assertNotIn('repair-startup', self.commands())
+            self.assertNotIn('cron-disable', self.commands())
+
+    def test_confirmed_repair_calls_existing_transaction(self):
+        p = self.run_shell('repair_existing_deployment', 'y\n', self.stubs())
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout+p.stderr)
+        self.assertIn('repair-startup', self.commands())
+        self.assertNotIn('restart docker', self.commands())
+
+    def test_failed_preflight_stops_before_confirmation_or_mutation(self):
+        p = self.run_shell('repair_existing_deployment', 'y\n', self.stubs()+'''
+existing_deployment_tool() { record existing "$@"; return 1; }
+''')
+        self.assertNotEqual(p.returncode, 0)
+        self.assertNotIn('repair-startup', self.commands())
+
+    def test_cli_repair_uses_management_lock_and_blocks_during_migration(self):
+        p = self.run_shell('main --repair-existing', 'n\n', self.stubs())
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout+p.stderr)
+        p = self.run_shell('mkdir -p "$FIREWALL_MIGRATION_STATE"; main --repair-existing', 'y\n', self.stubs())
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 75)
+
+    def test_each_cron_confirmation_reads_user_input(self):
+        p = self.run_shell('repair_existing_deployment', 'y\ny\ny\n', self.stubs()+'''
+maintenance_tool() { return 0; }
+existing_deployment_tool() {
+ record existing "$@"
+ case "$1" in
+ plan) printf '{"cron_risks":[],"restore_services":[]}' ;;
+ cron-list) printf '[{"index":1,"family":"IPv4"},{"index":2,"family":"IPv6"}]' ;;
+ esac
+}
+''')
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout+p.stderr)
+        self.assertEqual(self.commands().count('existing cron-disable'), 2)
+
+    def test_partial_cron_failure_still_attempts_restore(self):
+        p = self.run_shell('repair_existing_deployment', 'y\ny\n', self.stubs()+'''
+maintenance_tool() { return 0; }
+existing_deployment_tool() {
+ record existing "$@"
+ case "$1" in
+ plan) printf '{"cron_risks":[],"restore_services":[]}' ;;
+ cron-list) printf '[{"index":1,"family":"IPv4"}]' ;;
+ cron-disable) return 1 ;;
+ esac
+}
+''')
+        self.assertNotEqual(p.returncode, 0)
+        self.assertIn('existing cron-restore', self.commands())
+
+    def test_backup_root_is_dedicated_and_can_be_reused(self):
+        p = self.run_shell('repair_existing_deployment && repair_existing_deployment', 'y\ny\ny\ny\n', self.stubs()+'''
+existing_deployment_tool() {
+ record existing "$@"
+ case "$1" in
+ plan) printf '{"cron_risks":[],"restore_services":[]}' ;;
+ cron-list) printf '[{"index":1,"family":"IPv4"}]' ;;
+ esac
+}
+''')
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout+p.stderr)
+        self.assertEqual(self.commands().count('existing cron-disable'), 2)
+        self.assertIn('/existing-deployment/cron.', self.commands())

+"""Read-only decision snapshots and actually referenced kernel sets."""
+import ipaddress
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+from unittest.mock import patch
+
+import crowdsec_firewall_evidence as evidence
+
+
+class Evidence(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
+        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
+        self.config = Path(self.tmp.name)/'bouncer.yaml'
+        self.config.write_text('mode: iptables\napi_url: http://127.0.0.1:8080/\napi_key: private-canary\n')
+
+    def rows(self):
+        return [dict(value='198.51.100.7', type='ban', scope='Ip', duration='1h'),
+                dict(value='2001:db8::/64', type='ban', scope='Range', duration='1m'),
+                dict(value='192.0.2.1', type='ban', scope='Ip', duration='3s'),
+                dict(value='192.0.2.2', type='captcha', scope='Ip', duration='1h')]
+
+    def test_selects_applicable_long_lived_bans(self):
+        self.assertEqual(evidence.networks(self.rows()), {ipaddress.ip_network('198.51.100.7/32'), ipaddress.ip_network('2001:db8::/64')})
+
+    def test_coverage_uses_only_referenced_sets_and_accepts_supernets(self):
+        expected = evidence.networks(self.rows())
+        refs = {4: {'v4'}, 6: {'v6'}}
+        text = 'create v4 hash:net family inet\ncreate v6 hash:net family inet6\nadd v4 198.51.100.0/24\nadd unused 192.0.2.0/24\nadd v6 2001:db8::/48\n'
+        self.assertEqual(evidence.membership(expected, refs, text)['missing'], 0)
+        text = text.replace('add v4 ', 'add unused ')
+        self.assertEqual(evidence.membership(expected, refs, text)['missing'], 1)
+
+    def test_missing_or_wrong_family_set_is_not_healthy(self):
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            evidence.membership(set(), {4: {'v4'}, 6: set()}, '')
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            evidence.membership(set(), {4: {'v4'}, 6: set()}, 'create v4 hash:net family inet6\n')
+
+    def test_nomatch_cannot_count_as_enforced_ban(self):
+        for rows in ('add v4 198.51.100.7 nomatch\n', 'add v4 198.51.100.0/24\nadd v4 198.51.100.7 nomatch\n'):
+            with self.subTest(rows=rows), self.assertRaises(evidence.EvidenceError):
+                evidence.membership({ipaddress.ip_network('198.51.100.7/32')}, {4: {'v4'}, 6: set()}, 'create v4 hash:net family inet\n'+rows)
+
+    def test_custom_filters_are_explicitly_unknown(self):
+        for field in ('origins', 'scopes', 'scenarios_containing', 'scenarios_not_containing'):
+            with self.subTest(field=field):
+                with self.config.open('a') as out:
+                    out.write(f'{field}: [custom]\n')
+                with self.assertRaises(evidence.EvidenceError):
+                    evidence.live(self.config)
+
+    def test_snapshot_never_consumes_stream_or_discloses_credentials(self):
+        requests = []
+        class Response:
+            def __enter__(self): return self
+            def __exit__(self, *args): pass
+            def read(self, limit): return json.dumps(self_rows).encode()
+        self_rows = self.rows()
+        class Opener:
+            def open(self, request, timeout):
+                requests.append(request)
+                return Response()
+        with patch.object(evidence.urllib.request, 'build_opener', return_value=Opener()):
+            self.assertEqual(evidence.live(self.config), self_rows)
+        self.assertEqual(requests[0].full_url, 'http://127.0.0.1:8080/v1/decisions')
+        self.assertEqual(requests[0].get_method(), 'GET')
+        with patch.object(evidence.urllib.request, 'build_opener', side_effect=ValueError('private-canary')):
+            with self.assertRaises(evidence.EvidenceError) as error:
+                evidence.live(self.config)
+        self.assertNotIn('private-canary', str(error.exception))
+
+    def test_wait_retries_missing_but_has_deadline(self):
+        reports = [dict(state='pending', missing=1), dict(state='ok', missing=0)]
+        with patch.object(evidence, 'inspect', side_effect=reports), patch.object(evidence.time, 'sleep'):
+            self.assertEqual(evidence.wait(self.config, seconds=2)['state'], 'ok')
+        with patch.object(evidence, 'inspect', return_value=reports[0]), patch.object(evidence.time, 'monotonic', side_effect=[0, 5]):
+            self.assertEqual(evidence.wait(self.config, seconds=2)['state'], 'fault')

+"""TG alignment regressions; host commands and Telegram stay isolated."""
+import fcntl
+import importlib.util
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+from unittest.mock import patch
+
+import test_custom_bot_flow
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'lib'))
+
+
+class MaintenanceGuard(unittest.TestCase):
+    setUp = test_custom_bot_flow.ConfirmedFlows.setUp
+    telegram = test_custom_bot_flow.ConfirmedFlows.telegram
+    message = test_custom_bot_flow.ConfirmedFlows.message
+    callback = test_custom_bot_flow.ConfirmedFlows.callback
+    request = test_custom_bot_flow.ConfirmedFlows.request
+
+    def paths(self):
+        self.bot.MAINTENANCE_LOCK = str(self.base / 'maintenance.lock')
+        self.bot.MIGRATION_STATE = str(self.base / 'migration')
+
+    def confirmed(self):
+        self.paths()
+        yes, _ = self.request(self.message('/unban 203.0.113.8'))
+        return yes
+
+    def result_text(self):
+        return '\n'.join(payload.get('text', '') for _, payload in self.sent)
+
+    def test_sticky_lock_directory_allows_safe_shared_lock_but_not_private_reports(self):
+        from crowdsec_bot_runtime import maintenance_guard, MaintenanceRefused
+        from crowdbastion_ux import create_private
+        directory = self.base / 'shared-locks'
+        directory.mkdir()
+        directory.chmod(0o1777)
+        lock = directory / 'maintenance.lock'
+        migration = self.base / 'migration'
+        for existing in (False, True):
+            with self.subTest(existing=existing):
+                lock.unlink(missing_ok=True)
+                if existing:
+                    lock.write_text('')
+                    lock.chmod(0o644)
+                with maintenance_guard(lock, migration):
+                    with lock.open() as contender:
+                        with self.assertRaises(BlockingIOError):
+                            fcntl.flock(contender, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
+                with lock.open() as contender:
+                    fcntl.flock(contender, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
+        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
+            create_private(directory / 'report.txt', 'private')
+        directory.chmod(0o777)
+        with self.assertRaises(MaintenanceRefused):
+            with maintenance_guard(lock, migration):
+                self.fail('non-sticky writable directory accepted')
+        directory.chmod(0o1777)
+        lock.chmod(0o666)
+        with self.assertRaises(MaintenanceRefused):
+            with maintenance_guard(lock, migration):
+                self.fail('writable lock accepted')
+
+    def test_busy_main_lock_refuses_confirmed_mutation(self):
+        yes = self.confirmed()
+        with open(self.bot.MAINTENANCE_LOCK, 'w') as lock:
+            fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
+            with patch.object(self.bot, 'run_cmd') as command:
+                self.bot.handle_update(self.callback(yes))
+                command.assert_not_called()
+        self.assertIn('未执行', self.result_text())
+        self.assertIn('管理操作', self.result_text())
+        self.assertEqual(self.bot.get_action_store().unfinished(), [])
+
+    def test_unfinished_migration_refuses_mutation(self):
+        yes = self.confirmed()
+        Path(self.bot.MIGRATION_STATE).mkdir()
+        with patch.object(self.bot, 'run_cmd') as command:
+            self.bot.handle_update(self.callback(yes))
+            command.assert_not_called()
+        self.assertIn('迁移', self.result_text())
+        self.assertIn('未执行', self.result_text())
+
+    def test_completed_migration_permits_mutation_and_releases_shared_lock(self):
+        yes = self.confirmed()
+        directory = Path(self.bot.MIGRATION_STATE)
+        directory.mkdir()
+        (directory / 'committed').touch()
+        self.bot._HEALTH_COLLECTOR = ('old-cache', object())
+        with patch.object(self.bot, 'run_cmd', return_value='') as command:
+            self.bot.handle_update(self.callback(yes))
+            self.assertEqual(command.call_count, 1)
+        self.assertIsNone(self.bot._HEALTH_COLLECTOR)
+        with open(self.bot.MAINTENANCE_LOCK, 'r') as lock:
+            fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
+
+    def test_lock_symlink_is_refused_without_touching_target(self):
+        yes = self.confirmed()
+        target = self.base / 'foreign'
+        target.write_text('keep')
+        Path(self.bot.MAINTENANCE_LOCK).symlink_to(target)
+        with patch.object(self.bot, 'run_cmd') as command:
+            self.bot.handle_update(self.callback(yes))
+            command.assert_not_called()
+        self.assertEqual(target.read_text(), 'keep')
+        self.assertIn('未执行', self.result_text())
+
+    def test_unknown_migration_file_or_link_is_refused(self):
+        for kind in ('file', 'link'):
+            with self.subTest(kind=kind):
+                yes = self.confirmed()
+                directory = Path(self.bot.MIGRATION_STATE)
+                if kind == 'file':
+                    directory.write_text('unknown')
+                else:
+                    directory.symlink_to(self.base / 'absent')
+                try:
+                    with patch.object(self.bot, 'run_cmd') as command:
+                        self.bot.handle_update(self.callback(yes))
+                        command.assert_not_called()
+                finally:
+                    directory.unlink()
+
+
+class SharedHealth(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
+        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
+        self.base = Path(self.tmp.name)
+
+    def payload(self, checks=None):
+        from crowdbastion_health import summarize
+        checks = checks or {'core': 'ok', 'firewall_paths': 'fault', 'traffic': 'pending'}
+        return {'version': 1, 'checked_at': 1750000000.0,
+                'config_path': str(self.base / 'config.yaml'), 'checks': checks, 'required': [],
+                'summary': summarize(list(checks.items()), 1, 'overall', 'relay'),
+                'scope': '本地中转'}
+
+    def manager(self, payload, status=1):
+        path = self.base / 'manager with space.sh'
+        import shlex
+        path.write_text("printf '%s\\n' " + shlex.quote(json.dumps(payload)) + '\nexit ' + str(status) + '\n')
+        return path
+
+    def test_renderer_reports_round_trip_without_losing_required_observations(self):
+        from crowdbastion_health import structured_report
+        from crowdsec_bot_runtime import read_manager_report
+        for roles in ('', 'web', 'relay', 'remote', 'node', 'relay node', 'remote node'):
+            for state in ('ok', 'fault', 'missing', 'unknown'):
+                with self.subTest(roles=roles, state=state):
+                    records = [('core', 'required'), ('core', state),
+                               ('traffic', 'required'), ('traffic', 'pending')]
+                    status = 1 if state == 'fault' else 2
+                    payload = structured_report(records, status, 'overall', roles, str(self.base / 'config.yaml'))
+                    result = read_manager_report(self.manager(payload, status), payload['config_path'])
+                    self.assertEqual(result['summary'], payload['summary'])
+                    self.assertEqual(result['checks'], payload['checks'])
+
+    def test_collector_reads_canonical_firewall_and_node_observations(self):
+        import crowdsec_bot_health
+        payload = self.payload()
+        manager = self.manager(payload)
+        collector = crowdsec_bot_health.HealthCollector(payload['config_path'], manager_path=str(manager))
+        result = collector.collect()
+        self.assertEqual(result['checks'], payload['checks'])
+        self.assertEqual(result['summary']['verdict'], '发现问题，需要处理')
+        self.assertEqual(collector.collect(), result)
+
+    def test_invalid_or_mismatched_manager_report_cannot_fall_back_to_healthy(self):
+        import crowdsec_bot_health
+        payloads = [self.payload(), self.payload(), self.payload()]
+        payloads[0]['version'] = 99
+        payloads[1]['config_path'] = '/different/config.yaml'
+        payloads[2]['checks']['core'] = 'arbitrary-private-output'
+        for required in (None, {}, ['core', 'core'], ['unknown-key'], [['core']]):
+            payload = self.payload()
+            payload['required'] = required
+            payloads.append(payload)
+        for payload in payloads:
+            with self.subTest(payload=payload):
+                manager = self.manager(payload)
+                result = crowdsec_bot_health.HealthCollector(str(self.base / 'config.yaml'), manager_path=str(manager)).collect()
+                self.assertEqual(result['checks']['check'], 'unknown')
+                self.assertIn('未完成', result['summary']['verdict'])
+                self.assertNotIn('arbitrary-private-output', json.dumps(result))
+
+    def test_manager_timeout_returns_incomplete_without_exposing_output(self):
+        import crowdsec_bot_runtime
+        manager = self.base / 'manager.sh'
+        manager.write_text('echo private-diagnostic\nsleep 10\n')
+        with self.assertRaises((ValueError, RuntimeError, subprocess.SubprocessError)):
+            crowdsec_bot_runtime.read_manager_report(str(manager), str(self.base / 'config.yaml'), timeout=0.1)
+
+
+class CanonicalHealthCLI(unittest.TestCase):
+    from test_workflows import Workflows
+    setUp = Workflows.setUp
+    run_shell = Workflows.run_shell
+
+    def test_json_cli_uses_same_readonly_collection_without_leaking_details(self):
+        fixture = '''check_health_status_raw() {
+          NODE_ROLES=relay
+          printf 'private-diagnostic API Key: Q7k9xV1w\\n'
+          health_expect core; health_event core ok
+          health_expect firewall_paths; health_event firewall_paths fault
+          health_expect traffic; health_event traffic pending
+          return 1
+        }'''
+        result = self.run_shell('main --status-json', overrides=fixture)
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 1)
+        report = json.loads(result.stdout)
+        self.assertEqual(report['version'], 1)
+        self.assertEqual(report['config_path'], str(self.config / 'config.yaml'))
+        self.assertEqual(report['checks']['firewall_paths'], 'fault')
+        self.assertEqual(report['checks']['traffic'], 'pending')
+        self.assertIn('本地中转', report['scope'])
+        self.assertEqual(report['summary']['status'], 1)
+        self.assertNotIn('Q7k9xV1w', result.stdout + result.stderr)
+        import shlex
+        from crowdsec_bot_runtime import read_manager_report
+        manager = self.base / 'canonical-report.sh'
+        manager.write_text("printf '%s\\n' "+shlex.quote(result.stdout)+'\nexit 1\n')
+        accepted = read_manager_report(manager, report['config_path'])
+        self.assertEqual(accepted['summary'], report['summary'])
+        self.assertEqual(accepted['checks'], report['checks'])
+

+class BotDeployment(unittest.TestCase):
+    from test_workflows import Workflows
+    from test_custom_bot import BotService
+    setUp = Workflows.setUp
+    run_shell = Workflows.run_shell
+    commands = Workflows.commands
+    setup_bot = BotService.setup_bot
+
+    @property
+    def botroot(self):
+        return self.base / 'usr/local/lib/crowdsec-1key-bot'
+
+    def config_bytes(self):
+        return (self.config / 'crowdsec-1key-bot.json').read_bytes()
+
+    def test_conflict_scan_isolates_host_processes_and_still_detects_fixture_bot(self):
+        process = subprocess.Popen(['python3', '-c', 'import time; time.sleep(30)', 'crowdsec_bot.py'],
+                                   stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
+        try:
+            result = self.setup_bot()
+            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout+result.stderr)
+        finally:
+            process.terminate()
+            process.wait(timeout=5)
+        entry = self.base / 'proc/424242'
+        entry.mkdir(parents=True)
+        (entry / 'cmdline').write_bytes(b'python3\0/foreign/crowdsec_bot.py\0')
+        before = self.config_bytes()
+        result = self.setup_bot()
+        self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
+        self.assertIn('PID 424242', result.stdout+result.stderr)
+        self.assertEqual(self.config_bytes(), before)
+
+    def test_install_includes_canonical_runtime_and_paths(self):
+        result = self.setup_bot()
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout+result.stderr)
+        config = json.loads(self.config_bytes())
+        self.assertEqual(config['maintenance_lock'], str(self.base / 'run/lock/crowdsec-1key.lock'))
+        self.assertEqual(config['migration_state'], str(self.base / 'var/lib/crowdbastion/firewall-migration'))
+        manager = Path(config['manager_script'])
+        self.assertEqual(manager, self.botroot / 'runtime/current/manager.sh')
+        self.assertEqual(manager.read_bytes(), self.source.read_bytes())
+        env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(self.botroot), PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1')
+        result = subprocess.run(['python3', '-c', 'import crowdsec_bot_health, crowdsec_bot_runtime; print("self-contained")'],
+                                cwd='/', env=env, capture_output=True, text=True)
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+        self.assertIn('self-contained', result.stdout)
+
+    def test_version_check_detects_program_helper_and_runtime_drift(self):
+        self.assertEqual(self.setup_bot().returncode, 0)
+        config_before = self.config_bytes()
+        result = self.run_shell('custom_bot_code_status')
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout+result.stderr)
+        self.assertIn('一致', result.stdout)
+        for relative in ('crowdsec_bot.py', 'crowdsec_bot_health.py', 'runtime/current/lib/crowdbastion_health.py'):
+            path = self.botroot / relative
+            original = path.read_bytes()
+            try:
+                path.write_bytes(original + b'\n# drift\n')
+                result = self.run_shell('custom_bot_code_status')
+                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stdout+result.stderr)
+                self.assertIn('不同', result.stdout)
+            finally:
+                path.write_bytes(original)
+        self.assertEqual(self.config_bytes(), config_before)
+
+    def test_update_preserves_config_and_state(self):
+        self.assertEqual(self.setup_bot().returncode, 0)
+        config_before = self.config_bytes()
+        state = self.base / 'var/lib/crowdsec-1key-bot/synthetic-offset'
+        state.write_text('41')
+        (self.botroot / 'crowdsec_bot.py').write_text('# old code\n')
+        result = self.run_shell('update_custom_bot', 'y\n')
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout+result.stderr)
+        self.assertEqual(self.config_bytes(), config_before)
+        self.assertEqual(state.read_text(), '41')
+        self.assertEqual((self.botroot / 'crowdsec_bot.py').read_bytes(), (self.base / 'lib/crowdsec_bot.py').read_bytes())
+        self.assertEqual(self.run_shell('custom_bot_code_status').returncode, 0)
+
+    def test_update_cancel_keeps_code_config_and_service(self):
+        self.assertEqual(self.setup_bot().returncode, 0)
+        config_before = self.config_bytes()
+        installed = self.botroot / 'crowdsec_bot.py'
+        installed.write_text('# old code\n')
+        self.trace.write_text('')
+        result = self.run_shell('update_custom_bot', '\n')
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout+result.stderr)
+        self.assertEqual(installed.read_text(), '# old code\n')
+        self.assertEqual(self.config_bytes(), config_before)
+        self.assertNotIn('systemctl restart', self.commands())
+        self.assertNotIn('systemctl enable', self.commands())
+
+    def test_update_does_not_enable_or_start_an_inactive_disabled_service(self):
+        self.assertEqual(self.setup_bot().returncode, 0)
+        self.trace.write_text('')
+        override = '''systemctl() { record systemctl "$@";
+          case "$1" in
+            show) printf 'LoadState=loaded\\nFragmentPath=%s\\n' "$CUSTOM_BOT_UNIT";;
+            is-active) return 3;;
+            is-enabled) [[ "$*" == *--quiet* ]] || echo disabled; return 1;;
+          esac
+          return 0
+        }'''
+        result = self.run_shell('update_custom_bot', 'y\n', override)
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout+result.stderr)
+        for command in ('systemctl enable ', 'systemctl restart ', 'systemctl start '):
+            self.assertNotIn(command, self.commands())
+        self.assertIn('保留', result.stdout)
+
+    def test_failed_update_restores_helpers_config_and_runtime_link(self):
+        self.assertEqual(self.setup_bot().returncode, 0)
+        installed = self.botroot / 'crowdsec_bot_health.py'
+        installed.write_text('# old installed helper\n')
+        before = self.config_bytes()
+        current = self.botroot / 'runtime/current'
+        old_link = os.readlink(current)
+        self.trace.write_text('')
+        override = '''systemctl() { record systemctl "$@";
+          [[ "$1" != show ]] || printf 'LoadState=loaded\\nFragmentPath=%s\\n' "$CUSTOM_BOT_UNIT"
+          [[ "$1" != is-enabled ]] || echo enabled
+          [[ "$*" != 'restart crowdsec-1key-bot.service' ]]
+        }'''
+        result = self.run_shell('update_custom_bot', 'y\n', override)
+        self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
+        self.assertEqual(self.config_bytes(), before)
+        self.assertEqual(installed.read_text(), '# old installed helper\n')
+        self.assertEqual(os.readlink(current), old_link)
+        self.assertIn('systemctl restart crowdsec-1key-bot.service', self.commands())
+
+    def test_nonstandard_enabled_states_refuse_update_before_writes(self):
+        self.assertEqual(self.setup_bot().returncode, 0)
+        before = self.config_bytes()
+        current = self.botroot / 'runtime/current'
+        old_link = os.readlink(current)
+        for state in ('enabled-runtime', 'linked', 'linked-runtime', 'alias', 'static', 'indirect', 'generated', 'transient'):
+            with self.subTest(state=state):
+                self.trace.write_text('')
+                override = '''systemctl() { record systemctl "$@";
+                  case "$1" in
+                    show) printf 'LoadState=loaded\\nFragmentPath=%%s\\n' "$CUSTOM_BOT_UNIT";;
+                    is-enabled) [[ "$*" == *--quiet* ]] || echo %s;;
+                  esac
+                  return 0
+                }''' % state
+                result = self.run_shell('update_custom_bot', 'y\n', override)
+                self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout+result.stderr)
+                self.assertEqual(self.config_bytes(), before)
+                self.assertEqual(os.readlink(current), old_link)
+                self.assertNotIn('systemctl stop', self.commands())
+                self.assertNotIn('systemctl enable', self.commands())
+
+    def test_service_state_query_errors_refuse_update_before_writes(self):
+        self.assertEqual(self.setup_bot().returncode, 0)
+        before = self.config_bytes()
+        current = self.botroot / 'runtime/current'
+        old_link = os.readlink(current)
+        for failed_query in ('is-active', 'is-enabled'):
+            with self.subTest(query=failed_query):
+                self.trace.write_text('')
+                override = '''systemctl() { record systemctl "$@";
+                  case "$1" in
+                    show) printf 'LoadState=loaded\\nFragmentPath=%%s\\n' "$CUSTOM_BOT_UNIT";;
+                    is-active) %s;;
+                    is-enabled) return 1;;
+                  esac
+                }''' % ('return 1' if failed_query == 'is-active' else 'return 3')
+                result = self.run_shell('update_custom_bot', 'y\n', override)
+                self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
+                self.assertEqual(self.config_bytes(), before)
+                self.assertEqual(os.readlink(current), old_link)
+                self.assertNotIn('systemctl stop', self.commands())
+
+    def test_legacy_config_update_preserves_bytes_and_uses_bundled_manager(self):
+        self.assertEqual(self.setup_bot().returncode, 0)
+        filename = self.config / 'crowdsec-1key-bot.json'
+        document = json.loads(filename.read_text())
+        document.pop('manager_script')
+        filename.write_text(json.dumps(document, indent=3)+'\n')
+        before = filename.read_bytes()
+        result = self.run_shell('update_custom_bot', 'y\n')
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout+result.stderr)
+        self.assertEqual(filename.read_bytes(), before)
+        env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(self.botroot), PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1')
+        result = subprocess.run(['python3', '-c',
+            'import sys, crowdsec_bot as bot; bot.load_settings(sys.argv[1]); print(bot.MANAGER_SCRIPT)', str(filename)],
+            cwd='/', env=env, capture_output=True, text=True)
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+        self.assertEqual(result.stdout.strip(), str(self.botroot / 'runtime/current/manager.sh'))
+
+    def test_update_repairs_runtime_with_missing_helpers(self):
+        import shutil
+        self.assertEqual(self.setup_bot().returncode, 0)
+        shutil.rmtree(self.botroot / 'runtime/current/lib')
+        result = self.run_shell('custom_bot_code_status')
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 2, result.stdout+result.stderr)
+        result = self.run_shell('update_custom_bot', 'y\n')
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout+result.stderr)
+        self.assertEqual(self.run_shell('custom_bot_code_status').returncode, 0)
+
+    def test_version_check_refuses_foreign_runtime_pointer_even_if_code_matches(self):
+        import shutil
+        self.assertEqual(self.setup_bot().returncode, 0)
+        current = self.botroot / 'runtime/current'
+        foreign = self.base / 'foreign-release'
+        shutil.copytree(current.resolve(), foreign)
+        current.unlink()
+        current.symlink_to(foreign)
+        result = self.run_shell('custom_bot_code_status')
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 1, result.stdout+result.stderr)
+        self.trace.write_text('')
+        result = self.run_shell('update_custom_bot', 'y\n')
+        self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
+        self.assertNotIn('systemctl stop', self.commands())
+        self.assertEqual(current.resolve(), foreign)
+
+    def test_interrupted_update_restores_runtime_and_original_service(self):
+        self.assertEqual(self.setup_bot().returncode, 0)
+        current = self.botroot / 'runtime/current'
+        old_link = os.readlink(current)
+        before = self.config_bytes()
+        self.trace.write_text('')
+        override = '''cp() {
+          if [[ "${!#}" == "$CUSTOM_BOT_SCRIPT" && ! -f "$TRACE.interrupted" ]]; then
+            touch "$TRACE.interrupted"
+            kill -TERM "$BASHPID"
+          fi
+          command cp "$@"
+        }'''
+        result = self.run_shell('update_custom_bot', 'y\n', override)
+        self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
+        self.assertTrue(Path(str(self.trace)+'.interrupted').exists())
+        self.assertEqual(os.readlink(current), old_link)
+        self.assertEqual(self.config_bytes(), before)
+        self.assertIn('systemctl restart crowdsec-1key-bot.service', self.commands())
+
+    def test_stop_failure_attempts_to_recover_original_service(self):
+        self.assertEqual(self.setup_bot().returncode, 0)
+        self.trace.write_text('')
+        override = '''systemctl() { record systemctl "$@";
+          [[ "$1" != show ]] || printf 'LoadState=loaded\\nFragmentPath=%s\\n' "$CUSTOM_BOT_UNIT"
+          [[ "$1" != is-enabled ]] || echo enabled
+          [[ "$*" != 'stop crowdsec-1key-bot.service' ]]
+        }'''
+        result = self.run_shell('update_custom_bot', 'y\n', override)
+        self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
+        self.assertIn('systemctl restart crowdsec-1key-bot.service', self.commands())
+
+    def test_purge_removes_managed_runtime_but_refuses_foreign_runtime(self):
+        self.assertEqual(self.setup_bot().returncode, 0)
+        runtime = self.botroot / 'runtime'
+        result = self.run_shell('purge_custom_bot')
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout+result.stderr)
+        self.assertFalse(runtime.exists())
+
+    def test_purge_refuses_unknown_runtime_before_stopping_service(self):
+        self.assertEqual(self.setup_bot().returncode, 0)
+        runtime = self.botroot / 'runtime'
+        (runtime / '.crowdsec-1key-managed').unlink()
+        self.trace.write_text('')
+        result = self.run_shell('purge_custom_bot')
+        self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
+        self.assertTrue((self.botroot / 'crowdsec_bot.py').exists())
+        self.assertNotIn('systemctl disable', self.commands())
+
+    def test_null_maintenance_paths_are_rejected_by_config_validation(self):
+        self.assertEqual(self.setup_bot().returncode, 0)
+        filename = self.config / 'crowdsec-1key-bot.json'
+        original = json.loads(filename.read_text())
+        for key in ('maintenance_lock', 'migration_state'):
+            with self.subTest(key=key):
+                document = {**original, key: None}
+                filename.write_text(json.dumps(document))
+                result = self.run_shell('python3 "$CUSTOM_BOT_SCRIPT" --config "$CUSTOM_BOT_CONFIG" --check-config')
+                self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
+                self.assertNotIn('123:ABC', result.stdout+result.stderr)
+
+    def test_unknown_runtime_directory_refuses_setup_before_config_write(self):
+        runtime = self.botroot / 'runtime'
+        runtime.mkdir(parents=True)
+        (runtime / 'foreign').write_text('keep')
+        result = self.setup_bot()
+        self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
+        self.assertFalse((self.config / 'crowdsec-1key-bot.json').exists())
+        self.assertEqual((runtime / 'foreign').read_text(), 'keep')
+

+    unittest.main()

+"""User-visible progress, confirmations and private UX artifacts."""
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import shutil
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+import unittest
+from unittest.mock import patch
+
+import test_workflows
+import test_node_guidance
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT/'lib'))
+
+
+class Interaction(unittest.TestCase):
+    setUp = test_workflows.Workflows.setUp
+    run_shell = test_workflows.Workflows.run_shell
+    commands = test_workflows.Workflows.commands
+    roles = test_node_guidance.Guidance.roles
+    run_terminal = test_node_guidance.Guidance.run_terminal
+
+    def test_install_cancel_previews_changes_without_packages_or_writes(self):
+        before = (self.config/'config.yaml').read_bytes()
+        p = self.run_shell('install_crowdsec_core', '203.0.113.2\nn\n')
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout+p.stderr)
+        self.assertIn('本次变更预览', p.stdout)
+        self.assertIn('203.0.113.2', p.stdout)
+        self.assertIn('已有', p.stdout)
+        self.assertNotRegex(self.commands(), r'apt-get|curl|enable|restart|collections install')
+        self.assertEqual((self.config/'config.yaml').read_bytes(), before)
+
+    def test_invalid_admin_address_stops_before_installing_dependencies(self):
+        p = self.run_shell('install_crowdsec_core', 'bad-ip\ny\n')
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 1, p.stdout+p.stderr)
+        self.assertNotRegex(self.commands(), r'apt-get|curl|enable|restart')
+
+    def test_guidance_reads_service_state_without_claiming_deployment_pass(self):
+        self.roles('relay')
+        p = self.run_shell('guided_setup', '0\n', '''
+systemctl() { record systemctl "$@"; if [[ "$*" == 'is-active crowdsec' ]]; then echo inactive; return 3; fi; }
+''')
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout+p.stderr)
+        self.assertIn('服务未运行', p.stdout)
+        self.assertIn('待验证', p.stdout)
+        self.assertNotRegex(self.commands(), r'apt-get|enable|restart|reload')
+
+    def test_returning_from_website_bouncer_does_not_suggest_success(self):
+        p = self.run_shell('guided_website_setup', '1\n3\n0\n0\n')
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout+p.stderr)
+        self.assertIn('2. 私有连接地址（不适用', p.stdout)
+        self.assertIn('未检测到配置变化', p.stdout)
+        self.assertNotIn('操作已返回，请选择下一步', p.stdout)
+        self.assertNotRegex(self.commands(), r'apt-get|enable|restart|reload')
+
+    def test_successful_service_start_refreshes_report_once(self):
+        self.roles('relay')
+        p = self.run_terminal('interactive_health_check', '1\ny\n0\n', '''
+check_health_status_raw() {
+ record report-run
+ health_expect core
+ if [[ -f "$TRACE.started" ]]; then health_event core ok; else health_event core fault; fi
+ return 2
+}
+health_service_state() { echo inactive; }
+systemctl() { record systemctl "$@"; [[ "$1" != start ]] || touch "$TRACE.started"; }
+''')
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout)
+        self.assertEqual(self.commands().count('report-run'), 2, p.stdout+self.commands())
+        self.assertIn('自动复查', p.stdout)
+        self.assertNotIn('处理后请选择 3', p.stdout)
+
+    def test_declining_service_start_does_not_repeat_report(self):
+        self.roles('relay')
+        p = self.run_terminal('interactive_health_check', '1\nn\n0\n', '''
+check_health_status_raw() { record report-run; health_expect core; health_event core fault; return 1; }
+health_service_state() { echo inactive; }
+''')
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout)
+        self.assertEqual(self.commands().count('report-run'), 1)
+        self.assertNotIn('systemctl start', self.commands())
+
+    def test_runtime_sync_preserves_exact_cron_contents(self):
+        cron = self.base/'etc/cron.d/crowdsec-autoupdate'
+        runner = self.base/'usr/local/lib/crowdsec-1key/current/manager.sh'
+        cron.write_text(f'17 4 * * * root /bin/bash {runner} --update-hub >/dev/null 2>&1\n')
+        before = cron.read_bytes()
+        p = self.run_shell('sync_maintenance_runtime', 'y\n')
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout+p.stderr)
+        self.assertEqual(cron.read_bytes(), before)
+        self.assertTrue((self.base/'usr/local/lib/crowdsec-1key/current/manager.sh').is_file())
+        self.assertNotRegex(self.commands(), r'apt-get|restart|reload')
+
+    def test_runtime_sync_repairs_incomplete_managed_copy(self):
+        cron = self.base/'etc/cron.d/crowdsec-autoupdate'
+        runner = self.base/'usr/local/lib/crowdsec-1key/current/manager.sh'
+        cron.write_text(f'17 4 * * * root /bin/bash {runner} --update-hub\n')
+        setup = self.run_shell('install_maintenance_runtime')
+        self.assertEqual(setup.returncode, 0, setup.stdout+setup.stderr)
+        shutil.rmtree(runner.parent/'lib')
+        result = self.run_shell('sync_maintenance_runtime', 'y\n')
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout+result.stderr)
+        self.assertTrue((runner.parent/'lib/crowdbastion_ux.py').is_file())
+        self.assertIn('维护副本已同步', result.stdout)
+
+    def test_runtime_sync_refuses_unrelated_or_disabled_cron(self):
+        cron = self.base/'etc/cron.d/crowdsec-autoupdate'
+        runner = self.base/'usr/local/lib/crowdsec-1key/current/manager.sh'
+        for contents in ('17 4 * * * root /some/custom/command --flag\n',
+                         f'# 17 4 * * * root /bin/bash {runner} --update-hub\n'):
+            with self.subTest(contents=contents):
+                cron.write_text(contents)
+                result = self.run_shell('sync_maintenance_runtime', 'y\n')
+                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 1, result.stdout+result.stderr)
+                self.assertEqual(cron.read_text(), contents)
+                self.assertFalse(runner.exists())
+                self.assertNotIn('维护副本已同步', result.stdout)
+
+    def test_generator_creates_config_and_keeps_service_start_separate(self):
+        destination = self.base/'observer.json'
+        log = self.base/'access.log'
+        log.write_text('')
+        p = self.run_shell('generate_v2bx_observer_config',
+                           f'xray\n{log}\n+08:00\n198.51.100.5/32\n{destination}\ny\n')
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout+p.stderr)
+        config = json.loads(destination.read_text())
+        self.assertEqual(config['sources'][0]['relay_cidrs'], '198.51.100.5/32')
+        self.assertEqual(destination.stat().st_mode & 0o777, 0o600)
+        self.assertNotIn('systemctl enable', self.commands())
+        self.assertIn('持续记录菜单选 1', p.stdout)
+
+    def test_report_export_writes_private_redacted_artifact(self):
+        destination = self.base/'diagnosis.txt'
+        p = self.run_shell('export_health_details "$CAPTURE"', f'{destination}\n', '''
+CAPTURE=$(mktemp)
+printf '%s\\n' 'API_KEY=unique-test-secret' 'source 198.51.100.9' > "$CAPTURE"
+''')
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout+p.stderr)
+        report = destination.read_text()
+        self.assertNotIn('unique-test-secret', report)
+        self.assertNotIn('198.51.100.9', report)
+        self.assertEqual(destination.stat().st_mode & 0o777, 0o600)
+
+    def test_quick_actions_hold_maintenance_lock_for_mutating_menus(self):
+        p = self.run_shell('manage_quick_actions', '1\n4\n0\n', '''
+manage_firewall_maintenance() { record firewall-lock "${MAINTENANCE_LOCK_HELD:-missing}"; }
+manage_real_ip() { record realip-lock "${MAINTENANCE_LOCK_HELD:-missing}"; }
+''')
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout+p.stderr)
+        self.assertIn('firewall-lock true', self.commands())
+        self.assertIn('realip-lock true', self.commands())
+
+    def test_generator_path_is_reused_by_observer_menu(self):
+        destination = self.base/'observer.json'
+        log = self.base/'access.log'
+        log.write_text('')
+        answers = f'5\nxray\n{log}\n+08:00\n\n{destination}\ny\n1\n0\n'
+        p = self.run_shell('manage_v2bx_observer', answers, '''
+v2bx_observer_service() { record observer "$1" "$V2BX_OBSERVER_CONFIG"; }
+''')
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout+p.stderr)
+        self.assertIn(f'observer install {destination}', self.commands())
+
+    def test_config_generator_cancel_does_not_create_target(self):
+        target = self.base/'observer.json'
+        p = self.run_shell('generate_v2bx_observer_config', 'q\n')
+        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stdout+p.stderr)
+        self.assertFalse(target.exists())
+        self.assertNotRegex(self.commands(), r'apt-get|enable|restart|reload')
+
+
+class Artifacts(unittest.TestCase):
+    def setUp(self):
+        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
+        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
+        self.base = Path(self.tmp.name)
+
+    def ux(self):
+        import crowdbastion_ux
+        return crowdbastion_ux
+
+    def test_long_diagnostic_line_redacts_without_stalling(self):
+        result = subprocess.run(
+            [sys.executable, '-c', 'from crowdbastion_ux import redact; text="a"*131072; assert redact(text) == text'],
+            env=dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(ROOT/'lib'), PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1'),
+            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
+        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
+
+    def test_export_removes_secrets_addresses_and_terminal_controls(self):
+        raw = 'api_key: my-secret\npassword: "two words"\nAuthorization: Bearer abcdef\nsource=198.51.100.8 target=2001:db8::1\nhttps://user:pass@example.org/path?token=secret\n\\x1b[31m'.replace('\\x1b', '\x1b')
+        clean = self.ux().redact(raw)
+        for secret in ('my-secret', 'two words', 'abcdef', '198.51.100.8', '2001:db8::1', 'user:pass', 'token=secret', '\x1b'):
+            self.assertNotIn(secret, clean)
+
+    def test_report_masks_spaced_keys_cookies_and_control_split_credentials(self):
+        raw = 'API Key: Q7k9xV1w\napi_\x00key: B6m2zH8n\nCookie: session=D4s8cR3p\nSet-Cookie: session=F9a1jT5v\n'
+        clean = self.ux().redact(raw)
+        for secret in ('Q7k9xV1w', 'B6m2zH8n', 'D4s8cR3p', 'F9a1jT5v'):
+            with self.subTest(secret=secret):
+                self.assertNotIn(secret, clean)
+
+    def test_report_keeps_evidence_after_terminal_hyperlinks(self):
+        raw = '\x1b]8;;https://example.org\x1b\\diagnostic label\x1b]8;;\x1b\\\nnext check evidence\n'
+        clean = self.ux().redact(raw)
+        self.assertIn('diagnostic label', clean)
+        self.assertIn('next check evidence', clean)
+        self.assertNotIn('\x1b', clean)
+        self.assertNotIn('https://', clean)
+
+    def test_private_creation_refuses_existing_files_and_symlink_parents(self):
+        ux = self.ux()
+        path = self.base/'report.txt'
+        ux.create_private(path, 'first')
+        self.assertEqual(path.stat().st_mode & 0o777, 0o600)
+        with self.assertRaises((ValueError, OSError)):
+            ux.create_private(path, 'second')
+        self.assertEqual(path.read_text(), 'first')
+        link = self.base/'link'
+        link.symlink_to(self.base, target_is_directory=True)
+        with self.assertRaises((ValueError, OSError)):
+            ux.create_private(link/'other.txt', 'unsafe')
+        self.assertFalse((self.base/'other.txt').exists())
+
+    def test_private_artifacts_refuse_writable_ancestors_before_creating_directories(self):
+        ux = self.ux()
+        writable = self.base/'shared'
+        private = writable/'owned'
+        private.mkdir(parents=True, mode=0o700)
+        writable.chmod(0o777)
+        with self.subTest(artifact='report'):
+            with self.assertRaises((ValueError, OSError)):
+                ux.create_private(private/'report.txt', 'private diagnostic contents')
+            self.assertFalse((private/'report.txt').exists())
+        scope = dict(target='198.51.100.1', port=443, protocol='TCP', family='ip', hook='input')
+        with self.subTest(artifact='acceptance'):
+            with self.assertRaises((ValueError, OSError)):
+                ux.save_acceptance(writable/'new/acceptance.json', self.base, scope, 0)
+            self.assertFalse((writable/'new').exists())
+
+    def test_config_fingerprint_rejects_dangling_root_and_unreadable_subtrees(self):
+        ux = self.ux()
+        root = self.base/'config'
+        root.symlink_to(self.base/'missing', target_is_directory=True)
+        with self.subTest(root='dangling_link'):
+            with self.assertRaises((ValueError, OSError)):
+                ux.tree_fingerprint(root)
+        root.unlink()
+        root.mkdir()
+        with self.subTest(root='unreadable'):
+            with patch('os.scandir', side_effect=PermissionError('unreadable subtree')):
+                with self.assertRaises(PermissionError):
+                    ux.tree_fingerprint(root)
+
+    def test_runtime_fingerprint_detects_helper_changes_and_ignores_pycache(self):
+        ux = self.ux()
+        source = self.base/'source'
+        target = self.base/'target'
+        for folder, name in ((source, 'crowdbastion.sh'), (target, 'manager.sh')):
+            (folder/'lib').mkdir(parents=True)
+            (folder/name).write_text('main')
+            (folder/'lib/helper.py').write_text('one')
+        first = ux.code_fingerprint(source/'crowdbastion.sh')
+        self.assertEqual(first, ux.code_fingerprint(target/'manager.sh'))
+        (source/'lib/__pycache__').mkdir()
+        (source/'lib/__pycache__/helper.pyc').write_bytes(b'noise')
+        self.assertEqual(first, ux.code_fingerprint(source/'crowdbastion.sh'))
+        (target/'lib/helper.py').write_text('two')
+        self.assertNotEqual(first, ux.code_fingerprint(target/'manager.sh'))
+
+    def test_runtime_fingerprint_rejects_unreadable_helper_subtrees(self):
+        ux = self.ux()
+        manager = self.base/'manager.sh'
+        manager.write_text('#!/bin/bash\n')
+        (self.base/'lib').mkdir()
+        with patch('os.scandir', side_effect=PermissionError('unreadable helper subtree')):
+            with self.assertRaises(PermissionError):
+                ux.code_fingerprint(manager)
+
+    def test_compatibility_entry_has_same_runtime_fingerprint(self):
+        manager = self.base/'crowdbastion.sh'
+        manager.write_text('#!/bin/bash\n')
+        (self.base/'lib').mkdir()
+        (self.base/'lib/helper.py').write_text('print(1)\n')
+        legacy = self.base/'install-crowdsec-1panel.sh'
+        legacy.symlink_to(manager)
+        self.assertEqual(self.ux().code_fingerprint(legacy), self.ux().code_fingerprint(manager))
+
+    def test_report_masks_ipv6_followed_by_punctuation_or_scope(self):
+        text = self.ux().redact('连接来自 2001:db8::5.\n来源为 2001:db8::6%eth0\n')
+        self.assertNotIn('2001:db8', text)
+        self.assertNotIn('eth0', text)
+
+    def test_invalid_observer_source_leaves_no_config(self):
+        ux = self.ux()
+        path = self.base/'observer.json'
+        with self.assertRaises((ValueError, OSError)):
+            ux.observer_config(path, 'xray', '/missing/file', 'bad-zone', '', 'V2bX.service', write=True)
+        self.assertFalse(path.exists())
+
+    def test_valid_observer_config_passes_real_service_validator(self):
+        import crowdsec_v2bx_service as service
+        log = self.base/'access.log'
+        log.write_text('')
+        path = self.base/'observer.json'
+        self.ux().observer_config(path, 'xray', str(log), '+08:00', '203.0.113.0/24', 'V2bX.service', write=True)
+        result = service.load_config(path)
+        self.assertEqual(result['sources'][0]['timezone'], '+08:00')
+        self.assertEqual(result['sources'][0]['relay_cidrs'], '203.0.113.0/24')
+        self.assertEqual(path.stat().st_mode & 0o777, 0o600)
+
+    def test_acceptance_history_never_becomes_current_health_evidence(self):
+        ux = self.ux()
+        config = self.base/'config'
+        config.mkdir()
+        (config/'config.yaml').write_text('api: {}')
+        path = self.base/'acceptance.json'
+        scope = dict(target='198.51.100.1', port=443, protocol='TCP', family='ip', hook='input', source='1.1.1.1', control='8.8.8.8')
+        ux.save_acceptance(path, config, scope, 0, now=1000)
+        text = ux.acceptance_summary(path, config, now=1001)
+        self.assertIn('历史验收', text)
+        self.assertIn('人工确认', text)
+        self.assertIn('443', text)
+        self.assertIn('TCP', text)
+        self.assertIn('IPv4', text)
+        self.assertIn('过期', ux.acceptance_summary(path, config, now=1000+25*3600))
+        (config/'config.yaml').write_text('api: {server: {listen_uri: localhost:8081}}')
+        self.assertIn('配置变化', ux.acceptance_summary(path, config, now=1001))
+        self.assertNotIn('1.1.1.1', path.read_text())
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    unittest.main()
